- **finance-month-end-variance-analysis**: Runs month-end variance analysis in the only order that works: reconciliation first, explanation second, so that anything failing to tie out is reported as the finding rather than explained as a variance. Decomposes explained movement into price, volume, mix, timing, FX and one-off effects and refuses to produce commentary when the ledger, sub-ledger or prior-period figures were not supplied. Use at month-end or quarter-end close, when a P&L or cost line moved unexpectedly, when preparing the close pack, or when a reviewer asks why a number changed.
